_source line 505 · 2026-05-20T04:44:42.532Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested running Binoculars inference on qwen25_14b_temp09 data (4x5000 samples, d0-d3) using pythia-1.4b (observer) and pythia-410m (performer) on cuda:0 of gpu-6000pro-2 server. After inference, run a merged feature ablation combining 19 statistical features + 11 Binoculars features. Critical constraint: topk50 experiment on cuda:1 (PID 787051) must not be affected. Final deliverables: Binoculars per-calibration metrics, merged ablation (30-feat) accuracy, artifact registration.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Binoculars baseline (Hans et al., ICML 2024): cross-perplexity ratio between observer/performer LMs
   - Observer model: pythia-1.4b, Performer model: pythia-410m (same GPT-NeoX tokenizer family)
   - 5 evaluation methods: equal_width, coral, threshold, RF_single (1 feature), RF_multi (11 features)
   - 19 statistical features from `features_self_ref.jsonl` (computed with 14B model as self-reference)
   - 11 Binoculars features: bino_score, ppl_mean/var/median/p10/p90/skew, xppl_mean/var/median, bino_score_median
   - Merged ablation: 30-feature RF classifier (19 stat + 11 bino), 5-fold stratified CV
   - AutoDL environment constraints: network_turbo required, HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1, conda environment

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/binoculars_ordinal_eval.py` (550 lines)
     - Main Binoculars inference script; uses hardcoded CELL_SUBDIRS mapping; when data_dir points to temp09 dir, only "pythia_nucleus" (subdir=".") matches
     - Key constants: SEED=42, N_FOLDS=5, MAX_LENGTH=512, RF_N_ESTIMATORS=200
     - Outputs: JSON results + per-cell .npz files with features and labels

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/merged_ablation_14b_temp09.py` (uploaded to server)
     - Merged ablation script combining 19 stat + 11 bino features
     - Full code:
     ```python
     """Merged feature ablation: 19 stat features + 11 Binoculars features on 14B temp09."""
     import json
     import numpy as np
     from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier
     from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold
     from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, balanced_accuracy_score

     SEED = 42
     N_FOLDS = 5
     N_ESTIMATORS = 200

     STAT_FEATURES = [
         "mean_ppl", "var_ppl", "median_ppl", "p75_ppl", "p90_ppl",
         "skew_ppl", "kurt_ppl",
         "mean_surprisal", "var_surprisal", "median_surprisal",
         "p75_surprisal", "p90_surprisal",
         "type_token_ratio", "hapax_ratio",
         "bigram_entropy", "trigram_entropy",
         "rep_2gram", "rep_3gram", "rep_4gram",
     ]

     BINO_FEATURES = [
         "bino_score",
         "ppl_mean", "ppl_var", "ppl_median", "ppl_p10", "ppl_p90", "ppl_skew",
         "xppl_mean", "xppl_var", "xppl_median",
         "bino_score_median",
     ```

```json
{
```

```json
  "content": "# Binoculars Baseline + Merged Ablation: Qwen2.5-14B temp0.9\n\n**Cell**: qwen25_14b_temp09 (pythia_nucleus decoding)\n**Data**: 20,000 samples (5,000 per depth d0–d3)\n**Observer**: EleutherAI/pythia-1.4b | **Performer**: EleutherAI/pythia-410m\n**Date**: 2026-05-20\n\n## 1. Binoculars Baseline (5 calibration methods)\n\n| Method | #Features | 4-class Acc | d1–d3 Bal Acc |\n|--------|-----------|-------------|---------------|\n| equal_width | 1 (score) | 0.2943 ± 0.0029 | 0.3211 ± 0.0210 |\n| coral | 1 (score) | 0.2883 ± 0.0038 | 0.3844 ± 0.0051 |\n| threshold | 1 (score) | 0.3644 ± 0.0060 | 0.2595 ± 0.0078 |\n| RF_single | 1 (score) | 0.2917 ± 0.0063 | 0.2779 ± 0.0087 |\n| **RF_multi** | **11** | **0.4252 ± 0.0060** | **0.3717 ± 0.0076** |\n\nBest Binoculars method: RF_multi (11 token-level features), 4-class acc = **0.4252**.\n\n## 2. Merged Feature Ablation (RF 200 trees, 5-fold stratified CV)\n\n| Feature Set | #Features | 4-class Acc | d1–d3 Bal Acc |\n|-------------|-----------|-------------|---------------|\n| bino_single (bino_score only) | 1 | 0.2917 ± 0.0063 | 0.2779 ± 0.0087 |\n| bino_only | 11 | 0.4258 ± 0.0049 | 0.3724 ± 0.0071 |\n| stat_only (self-ref) | 19 | 0.5183 ± 0.0069 | 0.4559 ± 0.0083 |\n| **merged (stat + bino)** | **30** | **0.5419 ± 0.0093** | **0.4770 ± 0.0120** |\n\n## 3. Key Takeaways\n\n- **Stat features (19) dominate**: 0.5183 acc vs Binoculars RF_multi 0.4258 (+9.25pp).\n- **Merging helps**: 30-feat merged achieves 0.5419, a +2.36pp lift over stat-only and +11.61pp over bino-only.\n- **Binoculars single score is weak**: 0.2917 acc ≈ random (0.25), consistent with Binoculars not being designed for ordinal depth estimation.\n- **d1–d3 balanced accuracy** follows the same ranking: merged (0.477) > stat (0.456) > bino (0.372) > single (0.278).\n\n## 4. Files on Server\n\n- `results/binoculars_14b_temp09.json` — per-calibration Binoculars results\n- `results/pythia_nucleus_bino_features.npz` — 11 Binoculars features (20k samples)\n- `results/merged_ablation_14b_temp09.json` — merged ablation results\n- `data/qwen25_14b_temp09/features_self_ref.jsonl` — 19 stat features (20k samples)\n- `scripts/merged_ablation_14b_temp09.py` — ablation script\n"
}
```

全部完成。汇总：

**Binoculars baseline (14B temp09)**：RF_multi (11 features) 是最佳校准方法，4-class acc = **0.4252**。单 bino_score 仅 0.2917（≈随机）。

**Merged ablation (30 features)**：

| 特征集 | #feat | 4-class Acc | d1–d3 Bal Acc |
|--------|-------|-------------|---------------|
| bino_single | 1 | 0.2917 | 0.2779 |
| bino_only | 11 | 0.4258 | 0.3724 |
| stat_only | 19 | 0.5183 | 0.4559 |
| **merged** | **30** | **0.5419** | **0.4770** |

Stat features（19 维）远强于 Binoculars（11 维），合并后 30 维 RF 再涨 +2.4pp 到 0.5419。Artifact 已注册：`art_20260520_d47efe`。
